```
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Target-regenerated EAGLE data collection.
3	
4	This sends the prompt once, lets the target model generate, and relies on the
5	MiniCPM SGLang hook to save prompt-last + generated-token hidden/logit rows.
6	
7	Alignment contract:
8	  rid = e3os_norm_<idx>_<N> or e3os_full_<idx>_<N>
9	  request max_new_tokens = N + 1
10	  saved token_ids = [prompt_last] + generated rows that have target logits
11	
12	Normal mode stops at EOS and flushes the current buffer on request finish.
13	Full mode ignores EOS for a fixed ratio of requests and fills the full window.
14	"""
15	from __future__ import annotations
16	
17	import argparse
18	import json
19	import os
20	import time
21	from concurrent.futures import FIRST_COMPLETED, ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed, wait
22	from pathlib import Path
23	
24	import requests
25	import torch
26	from tqdm import tqdm
27	
28	
29	API_BASE = "http://127.0.0.1:30000"
30	BYTES_PER_SAVED_TOKEN = 25_400
31	
32	
33	def load_jsonl(path: Path, limit: int):
34	    out = []
35	    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
36	        for line in f:
37	            if not line.strip():
38	                continue
39	            out.append(json.loads(line))
40	            if limit > 0 and len(out) >= limit:
41	                break
42	    return out
43	
44	
45	def safe_rid(rid: str) -> str:
46	    return "".join(c if c.isalnum() or c in "._-" else "_" for c in rid)
47	
48	
49	def wait_pt(path: Path, timeout_s: float):
50	    deadline = time.time() + timeout_s
51	    while time.time() < deadline:
52	        if path.exists() and path.stat().st_size > 0:
53	            return True
54	        time.sleep(0.05)
55	    return False
56	
57	
58	def is_full_ignore_eos(idx: int, ratio: float) -> bool:
59	    if ratio <= 0:
60	        return False
61	    if ratio >= 1:
62	        return True
63	    bucket = (idx * 2654435761 % 10000) / 10000.0
64	    return bucket < ratio
65	
66	
67	def send_one(sample, idx: int, args):
68	    input_ids = sample.get("input_ids")
69	    if not isinstance(input_ids, list) or not input_ids:
70	        return False, "bad_input_ids"
71	
72	    full_ignore_eos = args.ignore_eos or is_full_ignore_eos(
73	        idx, args.full_ignore_eos_ratio
74	    )
75	    mode = "full" if full_ignore_eos else "norm"
76	    rid = f"{args.rid_prefix}_{mode}_{idx:07d}_{args.generate_tokens}"
77	    hook_path = args.collect_dir / f"{safe_rid(rid)}.pt"
78	    if hook_path.exists():
79	        hook_path.unlink()
80	
81	    try:
82	        r = requests.post(
83	            f"{args.api_base}/generate",
84	            json={
85	                "rid": rid,
86	                "input_ids": input_ids,
87	                "sampling_params": {
88	                    "temperature": args.temperature,
89	                    "max_new_tokens": args.generate_tokens + 1,
90	                    "ignore_eos": full_ignore_eos,
91	                },
92	            },
93	            timeout=args.request_timeout,
94	        )
95	    except Exception as e:
96	        return False, f"request_error:{type(e).__name__}"
97	
98	    if r.status_code != 200:
99	        return False, f"http_{r.status_code}:{r.text[:120]}"
100	    body = r.json()
101	    if "error" in body:
102	        return False, f"body_error:{str(body)[:120]}"
103	
104	    return True, {
105	        "sample": sample,
106	        "idx": idx,
107	        "input_ids": input_ids,
108	        "rid": rid,
109	        "mode": mode,
110	        "ignore_eos": full_ignore_eos,
111	        "hook_path": hook_path,
112	        "body": body,
113	    }
114	
115	
116	def finalize_one(ctx, args):
117	    """Convert the hook-side .pt into the final out_dir copy.
118	
119	    Deletes the hook .pt on every exit path (success or fail). The hook
120	    writer always emits a full file regardless of whether the sample
121	    passes finalize validation, so without this cleanup args.collect_dir
122	    grows in lockstep with args.out_dir (~50 MB/sample × 10k = 500 GB
123	    of duplicate data).
124	    """
125	    hook_path = ctx["hook_path"]
126	    try:
127	        return _finalize_one_inner(ctx, args)
128	    finally:
129	        try:
130	            hook_path.unlink()
131	        except FileNotFoundError:
132	            pass
133	
134	
135	def _finalize_one_inner(ctx, args):
136	    sample = ctx["sample"]
137	    idx = int(ctx["idx"])
138	    input_ids = ctx["input_ids"]
139	    hook_path = ctx["hook_path"]
140	    body = ctx["body"]
141	    mode = ctx["mode"]
142	    request_ignore_eos = bool(ctx["ignore_eos"])
143	
144	    if not wait_pt(hook_path, args.hook_timeout):
145	        output_ids = body.get("output_ids") or []
146	        finish_reason = body.get("meta_info", {}).get("finish_reason")
147	        if finish_reason is None:
148	            finish_reason = body.get("finish_reason")
149	        return (
150	            False,
151	            "no_hook_output:"
152	            f"output_ids={len(output_ids)} "
153	            f"finish_reason={finish_reason} "
154	            f"mode={mode} "
155	            f"ignore_eos={request_ignore_eos}",
156	        )
157	
158	    try:
159	        data = torch.load(hook_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
160	    except Exception as e:
161	        return False, f"load_error:{e}"
162	
163	    n = int(data["token_ids"].numel())
164	    expected = args.generate_tokens + 1
165	    if mode == "full":
166	        if n != expected:
167	            return False, f"bad_token_len:{n}!={expected}"
168	    else:
169	        if n < 1 or n > expected:
170	            return False, f"bad_token_len_norm:{n} not in [1,{expected}]"
171	    if data["assistant_mask"].shape[0] != n:
172	        return False, "bad_mask_len"
173	    if bool(data["assistant_mask"][0].item()):
174	        return False, "prompt_last_masked_trainable"
175	    if int(data["assistant_mask"][1:].sum().item()) != n - 1:
176	        return False, "generated_mask_not_all_trainable"
177	    for key in ("aux_hidden", "top_logit_values", "top_logit_indices", "target_logsumexp"):
178	        if data[key].shape[0] != n:
179	            return False, f"{key}_bad_len:{data[key].shape[0]}!={n}"
180	    if int(data["token_ids"][0].item()) != int(input_ids[-1]):
181	        return False, "prompt_last_token_mismatch"
182	
183	    data["source"] = sample.get("source", "")
184	    data["bucket"] = int(sample.get("bucket", len(input_ids)))
185	    data["n_prompt_tokens"] = len(input_ids)
186	    data["mode"] = mode
187	    data["request_ignore_eos"] = request_ignore_eos
188	    data["request_max_new_tokens"] = args.generate_tokens + 1
189	    data["n_generated_tokens"] = n - 1
190	    sample_doc_ids = sample.get("document_ids") or [0] * len(input_ids)
191	    prompt_last_doc = int(sample_doc_ids[-1]) if sample_doc_ids else 0
192	    data["document_ids"] = torch.full((n,), prompt_last_doc, dtype=torch.int32)
193	    if "output_ids" in body:
194	        output_ids = [int(x) for x in body["output_ids"]]
195	        saved_generated = n - 1
196	        if saved_generated > 0:
197	            saved = data["token_ids"][1:].cpu()
198	            prefix = torch.tensor(output_ids[:saved_generated], dtype=torch.long)
199	            if not torch.equal(saved, prefix):
200	                return False, "generated_prefix_mismatch"
201	        data["completion_token_ids"] = torch.tensor(
202	            output_ids[:saved_generated], dtype=torch.long
203	        )
204	        data["completion_token_ids_with_extra"] = torch.tensor(
205	            output_ids, dtype=torch.long
206	        )
207	        data["generated_output_ids"] = data["completion_token_ids_with_extra"]
208	        if len(output_ids) > saved_generated:
209	            data["extra_decode_token_id"] = int(output_ids[saved_generated])
210	
211	    completion_text = body.get("text", "")
212	    data["completion_text"] = completion_text
213	    data["response"] = completion_text
214	
215	    out_path = args.out_dir / f"{idx:06d}.pt"
216	    torch.save(data, out_path)
217	    return True, {
218	        "bytes": out_path.stat().st_size,
219	        "tokens": n,
220	    }
221	
222	
223	def collect_one(sample, idx: int, args):
224	    success, payload = send_one(sample, idx, args)
225	    if not success:
226	        return success, payload
227	    return finalize_one(payload, args)
228	
229	
230	def format_eta(seconds: float) -> str:
231	    seconds = max(0, int(seconds))
232	    h, rem = divmod(seconds, 3600)
233	    m, s = divmod(rem, 60)
234	    if h:
235	        return f"{h:d}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}"
236	    return f"{m:02d}:{s:02d}"
237	
238	
239	def disk_free_bytes(path: Path) -> int:
240	    target = path
241	    while not target.exists() and target != target.parent:
242	        target = target.parent
243	    st = os.statvfs(target)
244	    return st.f_bavail * st.f_frsize
245	
246	
247	def main():
248	    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
249	    p.add_argument("--in", dest="in_path", type=Path, required=True)
250	    p.add_argument("--out_dir", type=Path, required=True)
251	    p.add_argument("--collect_dir", type=Path, default=Path("/tmp/eagle_target_regen"))
252	    p.add_argument("--api-base", default=API_BASE)
253	    p.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=0)
254	    p.add_argument("--start", type=int, default=0)
255	    p.add_argument(
256	        "--generate-tokens",
257	        type=int,
258	        default=128,
259	        help="generated tokens used for training; saved sequence length is generate_tokens + 1",
260	    )
261	    p.add_argument("--temperature", type=float, default=0.0)
262	    p.add_argument(
263	        "--ignore-eos",
264	        action="store_true",
265	        help="force all requests into full ignore-eos mode; validation only",
266	    )
267	    p.add_argument(
268	        "--full-ignore-eos-ratio",
269	        type=float,
270	        default=0.10,
271	        help="deterministic ratio of requests that ignore EOS and fill the full window",
272	    )
273	    p.add_argument("--concurrency", type=int, default=1)
274	    p.add_argument("--rid-prefix", default="e3os")
275	    p.add_argument("--request-timeout", type=float, default=1200)
276	    p.add_argument("--hook-timeout", type=float, default=30)
277	    p.add_argument("--expected-tps", type=float, default=2000.0)
278	    p.add_argument("--min-free-gb", type=float, default=80.0)
279	    p.add_argument("--skip-disk-check", action="store_true")
280	    p.add_argument(
281	        "--coupled-finalize",
282	        action="store_true",
283	        help="legacy validation mode: request worker waits for hook output and saves",
284	    )
285	    args = p.parse_args()
286	
287	    args.out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
288	    args.collect_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
289	
290	    samples = load_jsonl(args.in_path, args.limit)
291	    n_plan = len(samples)
292	    saved_len = args.generate_tokens + 1
293	    est_bytes = int(n_plan * saved_len * BYTES_PER_SAVED_TOKEN)
294	    free_bytes = disk_free_bytes(args.out_dir)
295	    reserve_bytes = int(args.min_free_gb * 1024**3)
296	    est_hours = n_plan * saved_len / max(args.expected_tps, 1e-6) / 3600
297	    print(json.dumps({
298	        "plan_samples": n_plan,
299	        "saved_len_per_sample": saved_len,
300	        "request_max_new_tokens": saved_len,
301	        "concurrency": args.concurrency,
302	        "full_ignore_eos_ratio": args.full_ignore_eos_ratio,
303	        "force_ignore_eos": args.ignore_eos,
304	        "expected_tps": args.expected_tps,
305	        "estimated_hours": round(est_hours, 2),
306	        "estimated_storage_gb": round(est_bytes / 1024**3, 1),
307	        "free_gb": round(free_bytes / 1024**3, 1),
308	        "min_free_gb_after": args.min_free_gb,
309	    }, ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)
310	    if not args.skip_disk_check and free_bytes - est_bytes < reserve_bytes:
311	        raise SystemExit(
312	            f"insufficient disk: need est {est_bytes / 1024**3:.1f} GiB + "
313	            f"reserve {args.min_free_gb:.1f} GiB, free {free_bytes / 1024**3:.1f} GiB"
314	        )
315	
316	    ok = 0
317	    req_done = 0
318	    failures = []
319	    done_tokens = 0
320	    done_bytes = 0
321	    started = time.time()
322	
323	    def mark_success(payload):
324	        nonlocal ok, done_tokens, done_bytes
325	        ok += 1
326	        done_tokens += int(payload.get("tokens", saved_len))
327	        done_bytes += int(payload.get("bytes", 0))
328	
329	    def update_postfix(pbar, *, req_inflight=0, finalizing=0):
330	        elapsed = max(time.time() - started, 1e-6)
331	        tok_s = done_tokens / elapsed
332	        remaining = max(0, n_plan - ok - len(failures))
333	        eta = remaining * saved_len / max(tok_s, 1e-6)
334	        pbar.set_postfix(
335	            ok=ok,
336	            req_done=req_done,
337	            req_inflight=req_inflight,
338	            finalizing=finalizing,
339	            fail=len(failures),
340	            tok_s=f"{tok_s:.0f}",
341	            eta=format_eta(eta),
342	            gb=f"{done_bytes / 1024**3:.1f}",
343	        )
344	
345	    if args.concurrency <= 1:
346	        pbar = tqdm(samples, desc="target_regen collect", unit="sample")
347	        for local_i, sample in enumerate(pbar):
348	            idx = args.start + local_i
349	            success, payload = collect_one(sample, idx, args)
350	            if success:
351	                mark_success(payload)
352	            else:
353	                failures.append((idx, payload))
354	                tqdm.write(f"fail idx={idx}: {payload}")
355	            update_postfix(pbar)
356	    elif args.coupled_finalize:
357	        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.concurrency) as ex:
358	            futures = {
359	                ex.submit(collect_one, sample, args.start + local_i, args): args.start
360	                + local_i
361	                for local_i, sample in enumerate(samples)
362	            }
363	            pbar = tqdm(
364	                as_completed(futures),
365	                total=len(futures),
366	                desc="target_regen collect",
367	                unit="sample",
368	            )
369	            for fut in pbar:
370	                idx = futures[fut]
371	                try:
372	                    success, payload = fut.result()
373	                except Exception as e:
374	                    success, payload = False, f"worker_error:{type(e).__name__}:{e}"
375	                if success:
376	                    mark_success(payload)
377	                else:
378	                    failures.append((idx, payload))
379	                    tqdm.write(f"fail idx={idx}: {payload}")
380	                update_postfix(pbar)
381	    else:
382	        req_workers = args.concurrency
383	        save_workers = max(1, min(args.concurrency, 8))
384	        next_local_i = 0
385	        pending_req = {}
386	        pending_save = {}
387	
388	        def submit_next_request(ex):
389	            nonlocal next_local_i
390	            if next_local_i >= len(samples):
391	                return
392	            idx = args.start + next_local_i
393	            fut = ex.submit(send_one, samples[next_local_i], idx, args)
394	            pending_req[fut] = idx
395	            next_local_i += 1
396	
397	        pbar = tqdm(total=len(samples), desc="target_regen collect", unit="sample")
398	        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=req_workers) as req_ex, ThreadPoolExecutor(
399	            max_workers=save_workers
400	        ) as save_ex:
401	            for _ in range(min(req_workers, len(samples))):
402	                submit_next_request(req_ex)
403	
404	            while pending_req or pending_save:
405	                wait_set = set(pending_req) | set(pending_save)
406	                done, _ = wait(wait_set, return_when=FIRST_COMPLETED)
407	                for fut in done:
408	                    if fut in pending_req:
409	                        idx = pending_req.pop(fut)
410	                        submit_next_request(req_ex)
411	                        req_done += 1
412	                        try:
413	                            success, payload = fut.result()
414	                        except Exception as e:
415	                            success, payload = (
416	                                False,
417	                                f"request_worker_error:{type(e).__name__}:{e}",
418	                            )
419	                        if success:
420	                            save_fut = save_ex.submit(finalize_one, payload, args)
421	                            pending_save[save_fut] = idx
422	                        else:
423	                            failures.append((idx, payload))
424	                            tqdm.write(f"fail idx={idx}: {payload}")
425	                            pbar.update(1)
426	                            update_postfix(
427	                                pbar,
428	                                req_inflight=len(pending_req),
429	                                finalizing=len(pending_save),
430	                            )
431	                        continue
432	
433	                    idx = pending_save.pop(fut)
434	                    try:
435	                        success, payload = fut.result()
436	                    except Exception as e:
437	                        success, payload = (
438	                            False,
439	                            f"save_worker_error:{type(e).__name__}:{e}",
440	                        )
441	                    if success:
442	                        mark_success(payload)
443	                    else:
444	                        failures.append((idx, payload))
445	                        tqdm.write(f"fail idx={idx}: {payload}")
446	                    pbar.update(1)
447	                    update_postfix(
448	                        pbar,
449	                        req_inflight=len(pending_req),
450	                        finalizing=len(pending_save),
451	                    )
452	        pbar.close()
453	
454	    elapsed = max(time.time() - started, 1e-6)
455	    print(json.dumps({
456	        "ok": ok,
457	        "fail": len(failures),
458	        "elapsed_sec": round(elapsed, 2),
459	        "avg_saved_tokens_per_sec": round(done_tokens / elapsed, 2),
460	        "written_gb": round(done_bytes / 1024**3, 2),
461	        "failures": failures[:20],
462	    }, ensure_ascii=False))
463	    if failures:
464	        raise SystemExit(1)
465	
466	
467	if __name__ == "__main__":
468	    main()
469
```

```
6	# EAGLE target-regenerated collection server.

9	# CUDA graph stays enabled so collection follows the production decode path.
10	# Defaults below are the formal collection settings.
```

```
38	def _get_state_weight(state: dict[str, torch.Tensor], key: str) -> torch.Tensor:
39	    if key not in state:
40	        raise KeyError(f"checkpoint is missing {key}")
41	    return state[key].to(torch.bfloat16)

43	
44	def _load_v4_d2t_diff(vocab_cache_path: Path) -> torch.Tensor:
45	    cache = torch.load(vocab_cache_path, weights_only=True, map_location="cpu")
46	    if "selected_ids" in cache:
47	        selected_ids = cache["selected_ids"].long()
48	        if selected_ids.numel() != DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE:
49	            raise ValueError(
50	                f"selected_ids has {selected_ids.numel()} entries, expected {DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE}"
51	            )
52	        return selected_ids - torch.arange(DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE, dtype=torch.long)
53	
54	    d2t = cache["d2t"].long()
55	    if d2t.numel() != DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE:
56	        raise ValueError(f"d2t has {d2t.numel()} entries, expected {DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE}")
57	    # v4 cache stores diff-format d2t. Keep this branch for robustness if the
58	    # cache is regenerated without selected_ids.
59	    if int(d2t.max()) < VOCAB_SIZE and int(d2t.min()) >= -DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE:
60	        return d2t
61	    return d2t - torch.arange(DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE, dtype=torch.long)
62	
63	
64	def _copy_tokenizer_files(target_model: Path, output_dir: Path) -> None:
65	    for fname in (
66	        "tokenizer.json",
67	        "tokenizer_config.json",
68	        "special_tokens_map.json",
69	        "tokenizer.model",
70	        "added_tokens.json",
71	    ):
72	        src = target_model / fname
73	        if src.exists():
74	            shutil.copy2(src, output_dir / fname)
75	            print(f"Copied {fname}")
76	
77	
78	def convert(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
79	    ckpt_path = Path(args.ckpt)
80	    vocab_cache_path = Path(args.vocab_cache)
81	    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)
82	    target_model = Path(args.target_model)
83	
84	    print(f"Loading checkpoint: {ckpt_path}")
85	    ckpt = torch.load(ckpt_path, weights_only=True, map_location="cpu")
86	    state = ckpt["model_state_dict"]
87	    train_cfg = ckpt.get("config", {})
88	    aux_layers = train_cfg.get("aux_layers", [1, 10, 22])
89	    rope_theta = float(train_cfg.get("rope_theta", 144000.0))
90	    aoi_cap = int(train_cfg.get("aoi_cap", 144000))
91	    ttt_steps = int(train_cfg.get("ttt_steps", 3))
92	
93	    print(
94	        "Checkpoint metadata: "
95	        f"step={ckpt.get('global_step', 'n/a')} "
96	        f"best_ood0={ckpt.get('best_ood0', 'n/a')} "
97	        f"aux_layers={aux_layers} rope_theta={rope_theta:g}"
98	    )
99	
100	    d2t_diff = _load_v4_d2t_diff(vocab_cache_path)
101	
102	    def get(key: str) -> torch.Tensor:
103	        return _get_state_weight(state, key)
104	
105	    tensors: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
106	
107	    # v4 keeps fc and lm_head in bf16, matching the current SGLang exclude path.
108	    tensors["model.fc.weight"] = get("fc.weight")
109	
110	    print("Packing attention and MLP linear layers as NVFP4 ...")
111	    qkv_merged = torch.cat(
112	        [
113	            get("midlayer.self_attn.q_proj.weight"),
114	            get("midlayer.self_attn.k_proj.weight"),
115	            get("midlayer.self_attn.v_proj.weight"),
116	        ],
117	        dim=0,
118	    )
119	    add_nvfp4_tensors(
120	        tensors,
121	        "model.midlayer.self_attn.qkv_proj",
122	        qkv_merged,
123	        num_logical_weights=3,
124	    )
125	    del qkv_merged
126	
127	    add_nvfp4_tensors(
128	        tensors,
129	        "model.midlayer.self_attn.o_proj",
130	        get("midlayer.self_attn.o_proj.weight"),
131	    )
132	
133	    gate_up_merged = torch.cat(
134	        [
135	            get("midlayer.mlp.gate_proj.weight"),
136	            get("midlayer.mlp.up_proj.weight"),
137	        ],
138	        dim=0,
139	    )
140	    add_nvfp4_tensors(
141	        tensors,
142	        "model.midlayer.mlp.gate_up_proj",
143	        gate_up_merged,
144	        num_logical_weights=2,
145	    )
146	    del gate_up_merged
147	
148	    add_nvfp4_tensors(
149	        tensors,
150	        "model.midlayer.mlp.down_proj",
151	        get("midlayer.mlp.down_proj.weight"),
152	    )
153	
154	    tensors["lm_head.weight"] = get("lm_head.weight")
155	    tensors["model.midlayer.input_layernorm.weight"] = get(
156	        "midlayer.input_emb_norm.weight"
157	    )
158	    tensors["model.midlayer.hidden_norm.weight"] = get("midlayer.hidden_norm.weight")
159	    tensors["model.midlayer.post_attention_layernorm.weight"] = get(
160	        "midlayer.post_attention_layernorm.weight"
161	    )
162	    tensors["model.norm.weight"] = get("norm.weight")
163	    tensors["d2t"] = d2t_diff
164	
165	    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
166	    save_file(tensors, output_dir / "model.safetensors")
167	    print(f"Saved {len(tensors)} tensors to {output_dir / 'model.safetensors'}")
168	
169	    config = {
170	        "architectures": ["LlamaForCausalLMEagle3"],
171	        "model_type": "llama",
172	        "hidden_size": HIDDEN_SIZE,
173	        "intermediate_size": INTERMEDIATE_SIZE,
174	        "num_hidden_layers": 1,
175	        "num_attention_heads": NUM_HEADS,
176	        "num_key_value_heads": NUM_KV_HEADS,
177	        "head_dim": HEAD_DIM,
178	        "hidden_act": "silu",
179	        "max_position_embeddings": max(524288, aoi_cap),
180	        "rms_norm_eps": RMS_NORM_EPS,
181	        "vocab_size": VOCAB_SIZE,
182	        "draft_vocab_size": DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE,
183	        "target_hidden_size": HIDDEN_SIZE,
184	        "scale_emb": 12,
185	        "tie_word_embeddings": False,
186	        "torch_dtype": "bfloat16",
187	        "rope_theta": rope_theta,
188	        "eagle_config": {

190	            "eagle_aux_hidden_state_layer_ids": aux_layers,
191	            "aoi_cap": aoi_cap,
192	            "ttt_steps_trained": ttt_steps,
193	            "source": "eagle-v4",
194	        },
195	    }
196	    with (output_dir / "config.json").open("w") as f:
197	        json.dump(config, f, indent=2)
198	    print("Saved config.json")
199	
200	    hf_quant_cfg = {
201	        "producer": {"name": "eagle-v4-fp4-qat", "version": "1.0"},
202	        "quantization": {
203	            "quant_algo": "NVFP4",
204	            "kv_cache_quant_algo": None,
205	            "group_size": FP4_GROUP_SIZE,
206	            "has_zero_point": False,
207	            "pre_quant_scale": False,
208	            "exclude_modules": ["model.fc", "lm_head"],
209	        },
210	    }
211	    with (output_dir / "hf_quant_config.json").open("w") as f:
212	        json.dump(hf_quant_cfg, f, indent=2)
213	    print("Saved hf_quant_config.json")
214

216	        "source_checkpoint": str(ckpt_path),
217	        "source_vocab_cache": str(vocab_cache_path),
```

```
1	"""Sequence packing for SALA EAGLE-3 draft training.
2	
3	Pack multiple variable-length samples into one fixed-budget sequence to
4	eliminate padding waste. Each segment is fully isolated:
5	
6	  * Per-segment ``document_ids`` (0, 1, 2, ...). Cross-doc attention mask in
7	    the trainer blocks attention across segment boundaries.
8	  * Per-segment AOI sink + offset, so each segment's RoPE positions look
9	    independent (no global anchor on a packed sequence).
10	  * Per-segment tail masking. The trainer's TTT step k>0 left-shifts inputs
11	    by ``k`` positions, which on packed sequences pulls tokens from the
12	    next segment into the current segment's tail. We zero out the
13	    ``assistant_mask`` for the last ``ttt_steps`` positions of each segment
14	    so those polluted positions do not contribute to the loss.
15	
16	Public surface:
17	    index_lengths(data_dir, cache_path, force=False)
18	    pack_samples(samples, max_len, min_seg_len=64)
19	    aoi_position_ids_packed(seq_len, batch_size, document_ids, device,
20	                            training, aoi_cap, aoi_max_sink)
21	    mask_segment_tail(assistant_mask, document_ids, n_pad)
22	    make_packed_batch(samples, max_len, ttt_steps, device, dtype, min_seg_len=64)
23	    PackedFileSampler(files, lengths, max_len, min_seg_len)
24	"""
25	from __future__ import annotations
26	
27	import json
28	import multiprocessing as mp
29	import os
30	import random
31	import time
32	from pathlib import Path
33	
34	import torch
35	
36	
37	# ── Length index ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
38	
39	def _index_one(path_str: str) -> tuple[str, int | None, str | None]:
40	    """Single-file worker: returns (name, n_tokens, error_or_None).
41	
42	    Defined at module top level so multiprocessing.Pool can pickle it.
43	    """
44	    p = Path(path_str)
45	    try:
46	        d = torch.load(p, weights_only=True, map_location="cpu")
47	        return p.name, int(d["token_ids"].shape[0]), None
48	    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
49	        return p.name, None, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"
50	
51	
52	def index_lengths(data_dir: Path, cache_path: Path,
53	                  force: bool = False, verbose: bool = True,
54	                  num_workers: int | None = None) -> dict[str, int]:
55	    """Build {filename: token_ids length} index, persisted as JSON.
56	
57	    Loads every .pt once and records its ``token_ids`` length. Uses a
58	    multiprocessing pool to parallelize the torch.load — single-process
59	    runs at ~50 file/s due to I/O, multi-process scales linearly until
60	    storage saturates. Subsequent calls short-circuit on the cache; new
61	    files get filled in incrementally.
62	
63	    num_workers default = min(32, os.cpu_count() // 2). The bottleneck is
64	    storage IOPS, not CPU; oversubscribing has no benefit.
65	    """
66	    cache_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
67	    cached: dict[str, int] = {}
68	    if cache_path.exists() and not force:
69	        try:
70	            cached = json.loads(cache_path.read_text())
71	        except Exception:
72	            cached = {}
73	
74	    files = sorted(data_dir.glob("*.pt"))
75	    missing = [f for f in files if f.name not in cached]
76	    if not missing:
77	        return cached
78	
79	    if num_workers is None:
80	        num_workers = min(32, max(2, (os.cpu_count() or 4) // 2))
81	
82	    if verbose:
83	        print(f"[packing.index_lengths] {data_dir}: {len(missing)} new files to scan "
84	              f"({len(cached)} cached, {len(files)} total, workers={num_workers})")
85	    t0 = time.time()
86	    n_done = 0
87	    n_skip = 0
88	
89	    # fork is fine here: workers only do torch.load(map_location="cpu") and
90	    # never touch CUDA, so fork-time CUDA state inheritance is harmless. spawn
91	    # would re-import the parent's __main__ (which tries to rebuild the full
92	    # model + optimizer in every worker on every TTT step — many minutes lost
93	    # before the first index even completes).
94	    ctx = mp.get_context("fork")
95	    chunksize = max(1, len(missing) // (num_workers * 16) or 1)
96	    paths = [str(f) for f in missing]
97	
98	    with ctx.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:
99	        for name, n, err in pool.imap_unordered(_index_one, paths, chunksize=chunksize):
100	            n_done += 1
101	            if n is None:
102	                n_skip += 1
103	                if verbose:
104	                    print(f"  skip {name}: {err}")
105	                continue
106	            cached[name] = n
107	            if verbose and n_done % 1000 == 0:
108	                rate = n_done / (time.time() - t0)
109	                print(f"  scanned {n_done}/{len(missing)} ({time.time()-t0:.1f}s, {rate:.0f}/s)")
110	
111	    cache_path.write_text(json.dumps(cached))
112	    if verbose:
113	        print(f"[packing.index_lengths] wrote {cache_path} ({len(cached)} entries, "
114	              f"{n_skip} skipped, {time.time()-t0:.1f}s)")
115	    return cached
116	
117	
118	# ── Pack one bin from a list of loaded samples ─────────────────────────
119	
120	PACK_KEYS = (
121	    "token_ids",
122	    "aux_hidden",
123	    "top_logit_values",
124	    "top_logit_indices",
125	    "target_logsumexp",
126	    "assistant_mask",
127	)
128	
129	
130	def pack_samples(samples: list[dict], max_len: int,
131	                 min_seg_len: int = 64) -> dict | None:
132	    """Concatenate already-loaded samples along the seq axis.
133	
134	    Returns one packed dict (single-row, no batch dim) or None if no
135	    segment is admissible. Segments shorter than ``min_seg_len`` are
136	    silently dropped (they cannot afford the TTT tail masking).
137	
138	    Stops adding segments once the running length would exceed ``max_len``.
139	    The caller (bin packer) is responsible for choosing which samples to
140	    feed in; this routine does not reorder.
141	    """
142	    pieces = []
143	    total = 0
144	    for s in samples:
145	        n = int(s["token_ids"].shape[0])
146	        if n < min_seg_len:
147	            continue
148	        if total + n > max_len:
149	            continue
150	        pieces.append((s, n))
151	        total += n
152	    if not pieces:
153	        return None
154	
155	    out: dict = {}
156	    for k in PACK_KEYS:
157	        out[k] = torch.cat([s[k] for s, _ in pieces], dim=0)
158	
159	    seg_pieces = []
160	    for seg_idx, (_, n) in enumerate(pieces):
161	        seg_pieces.append(torch.full((n,), seg_idx, dtype=torch.int32))
162	    out["document_ids"] = torch.cat(seg_pieces, dim=0)
163	    out["_segment_lengths"] = [n for _, n in pieces]
164	    return out
165	
166	
167	# ── AOI per-segment positions ──────────────────────────────────────────
168	
169	def aoi_position_ids_packed(seq_len: int, batch_size: int,
170	                            document_ids: torch.Tensor,
171	                            device, training: bool,
172	                            aoi_cap: int, aoi_max_sink: int) -> torch.Tensor:
173	    """LongSpec AOI extended to packed sequences.
174	
175	    Each segment (run of equal ``document_ids[b]``) is treated as if it were
176	    a standalone shard:
177	        sink ~ U[0, aoi_max_sink]
178	        offset ~ U[0, aoi_cap - seg_len]
179	        pos = arange(seg_len), pos[sink:] += offset
180	    In eval / non-training mode positions are simply 0..S-1 globally.
181	    """
182	    if not training or seq_len >= aoi_cap:
183	        return (torch.arange(seq_len, device=device)
184	                .unsqueeze(0).expand(batch_size, -1).contiguous())
185	
186	    out = torch.zeros(batch_size, seq_len, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
187	    for b in range(batch_size):
188	        doc_row = document_ids[b]
189	        # Boundaries: indices where the segment starts.
190	        change = (doc_row[1:] != doc_row[:-1]).nonzero(as_tuple=True)[0]
191	        starts = [0] + (change + 1).tolist() + [seq_len]
192	        for i in range(len(starts) - 1):
193	            seg_start = starts[i]
194	            seg_end = starts[i + 1]
195	            seg_len = seg_end - seg_start
196	            if seg_len <= 0:
197	                continue
198	            sink = random.randint(0, aoi_max_sink)
199	            offset_max = max(0, aoi_cap - seg_len)
200	            offset = random.randint(0, offset_max) if offset_max > 0 else 0
201	            pos = torch.arange(seg_len, device=device)
202	            if sink < seg_len:
203	                pos[sink:] = pos[sink:] + offset
204	            out[b, seg_start:seg_end] = pos
205	    return out
206	
207	
208	# ── TTT tail mask ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
209	
210	def mask_segment_tail(assistant_mask: torch.Tensor,
211	                      document_ids: torch.Tensor,
212	                      n_pad: int) -> torch.Tensor:
213	    """Zero out the last ``n_pad`` positions of each segment.
214	
215	    A position p is "safe" iff for every k in [1, n_pad]:
216	        p + k is in-bounds AND document_ids[p + k] == document_ids[p].
217	    Otherwise after TTT left-shift by k the input at position p originates
218	    from a different segment (or out of bounds), so its supervision is
219	    polluted and we must drop it from the loss.
220	    """
221	    if n_pad <= 0:
222	        return assistant_mask
223	    am = assistant_mask.bool().clone() if assistant_mask.dtype != torch.bool \
224	        else assistant_mask.clone()
225	    B, S = document_ids.shape
226	    for k in range(1, n_pad + 1):
227	        valid = S - k
228	        if valid > 0:
229	            same = document_ids[:, :valid] == document_ids[:, k:k + valid]
230	            am[:, :valid] = am[:, :valid] & same
231	        # Positions in [S-k, S) cannot satisfy the "p+k in range" condition.
232	        if valid < S:
233	            am[:, max(valid, 0):S] = False
234	    return am
235	
236	
237	# ── Bin-packing dataset wrapper ────────────────────────────────────────
238	
239	class PackedFileSampler:
240	    """First-fit-decreasing bin packer over a static file list.
241	
242	    Each call to ``next_bin()`` returns a list of file paths whose
243	    ``token_ids`` lengths fit a single packed sequence of ``max_len``.
244	    Files shorter than ``min_seg_len`` are dropped at index time.
245	
246	    Internally the sampler keeps a shuffled list of (path, length) pairs
247	    and consumes them in FFD greedy order. When the pool empties it
248	    reshuffles. This is randomized but reproducible across epochs.
249	    """
250	
251	    def __init__(self, files: list[Path], lengths: dict[str, int],
252	                 max_len: int, min_seg_len: int = 64,
253	                 ttt_pad: int = 0, rng: random.Random | None = None):
254	        self.max_len = max_len
255	        self.min_seg_len = min_seg_len
256	        self.ttt_pad = ttt_pad
257	        self.rng = rng or random
258	        self._all = [
259	            (p, int(lengths[p.name]))
260	            for p in files
261	            if p.name in lengths and int(lengths[p.name]) >= min_seg_len
262	        ]
263	        if not self._all:
264	            raise RuntimeError("PackedFileSampler: no files >= min_seg_len after indexing")
265	        self._reset_pool()
266	
267	    def _reset_pool(self):
268	        pool = list(self._all)
269	        self.rng.shuffle(pool)
270	        # Sort by length descending — FFD prefers placing the largest first.
271	        pool.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
272	        self._pool = pool
273	
274	    def stats(self) -> dict:
275	        lens = [n for _, n in self._all]
276	        return {
277	            "n_files": len(self._all),
278	            "min_len": min(lens),
279	            "max_len": max(lens),
280	            "mean_len": sum(lens) / len(lens),
281	            "pool_remaining": len(self._pool),
282	        }
283	
284	    def next_bin(self) -> list[Path]:
285	        """Return file paths for one packed batch. Reshuffles when the pool
286	        empties without ever returning an empty bin (raises only if no
287	        single file fits — which would mean min_seg_len > max_len).
288	        """
289	        bin_files: list[Path] = []
290	        bin_filled = 0
291	        # Walk the pool once looking for files that fit.
292	        kept_indices: list[int] = []
293	        for i, (p, n) in enumerate(self._pool):
294	            if bin_filled + n <= self.max_len:
295	                bin_files.append(p)
296	                bin_filled += n
297	            else:
298	                kept_indices.append(i)
299	        # Compute the survivors (everything we didn't consume).
300	        new_pool = [self._pool[i] for i in kept_indices]
301	        # If the pool actually became smaller, this round made progress.
302	        if len(new_pool) < len(self._pool):
303	            self._pool = new_pool
304	        else:
305	            # Nothing fit (e.g. all remaining files exceed max_len).
306	            # Drop the first (largest leftover) so we don't loop.
307	            self._pool = self._pool[1:]
308	        if not self._pool:
309	            self._reset_pool()
310	        if not bin_files:
311	            # Single file exceeds max_len: take the smallest one anyway.
312	            small = min(self._all, key=lambda x: x[1])
313	            bin_files = [small[0]]
314	        return bin_files
315	
316	
317	# ── Top-level batch builder ────────────────────────────────────────────
318	
319	def make_packed_batch(samples: list[dict], max_len: int, ttt_steps: int,
320	                      device: str | torch.device, dtype: torch.dtype,
321	                      min_seg_len: int = 64,
322	                      pin_memory: bool = False) -> dict:
323	    """Build a (B=1) packed batch.
324	
325	    Tail masking is applied to ``assistant_mask`` on whichever device we
326	    end on. ``pin_memory=True`` is honored only when device==cpu (CUDA
327	    tensors are already in device memory).
328	
329	    Stream-separation pattern: caller passes ``device='cpu', pin_memory=True``
330	    inside a worker thread, then in the main thread does
331	    ``{k: v.to('cuda', non_blocking=True) for k, v in batch.items()}`` so
332	    H2D copy runs on PyTorch's default copy stream and overlaps with
333	    forward compute on the main stream.
334	    """
335	    packed = pack_samples(samples, max_len, min_seg_len=min_seg_len)
336	    if packed is None:
337	        raise RuntimeError("make_packed_batch: pack_samples returned None")
338	
339	    n_real = packed["token_ids"].shape[0]
340	    if n_real < max_len:
341	        pad_len = max_len - n_real
342	        for k, t in list(packed.items()):
343	            if k.startswith("_") or not torch.is_tensor(t):
344	                continue
345	            pad_shape = list(t.shape)
346	            pad_shape[0] = pad_len
347	            pad_value = 0
348	            pad = torch.full(pad_shape, pad_value, dtype=t.dtype)
349	            packed[k] = torch.cat([t, pad], dim=0)
350	        pad_doc = int(packed["document_ids"][n_real - 1].item()) + 1
351	        packed["document_ids"][n_real:] = pad_doc
352	        packed["assistant_mask"][n_real:] = False
353	
354	    # Build batch on CPU first (cheap dtype casts only), then move/pin.
355	    out = {
356	        "token_ids": packed["token_ids"].unsqueeze(0).long(),
357	        "aux_hidden": packed["aux_hidden"].unsqueeze(0).to(dtype),
358	        "target_logits_values": packed["top_logit_values"].unsqueeze(0).to(torch.float32),
359	        "target_logits_indices": packed["top_logit_indices"].unsqueeze(0).long(),
360	        "target_logsumexp": packed["target_logsumexp"].unsqueeze(0).to(torch.float32),
361	        "assistant_mask": packed["assistant_mask"].unsqueeze(0).bool(),
362	        "document_ids": packed["document_ids"].unsqueeze(0).long(),
363	        "is_packed": True,
364	    }
365	    # Apply TTT tail masking on CPU before the H2D copy so the GPU never
366	    # sees an unmasked tail (and so we can keep the .to() unconditional).
367	    out["assistant_mask"] = mask_segment_tail(
368	        out["assistant_mask"], out["document_ids"], n_pad=ttt_steps
369	    )
370	
371	    is_cpu_target = (str(device) == "cpu")
372	    if pin_memory and is_cpu_target and torch.cuda.is_available():
373	        for k, v in list(out.items()):
374	            if torch.is_tensor(v):
375	                out[k] = v.pin_memory()
376	    elif not is_cpu_target:
377	        for k, v in list(out.items()):
378	            if torch.is_tensor(v):
379	                out[k] = v.to(device, non_blocking=True)
380	    return out
381
```

```
1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	# Train SALA EAGLE-3 draft on the merged v2mix_20k dataset (10k base +
3	# 10k incremental, hard-linked under v2mix_20k/).
4	#
5	# This run uses every optimisation we landed in the recent refactor:
6	#   * NVFP4 forward GEMM on sm_120 (~+14% throughput vs bf16 STE)
7	#   * sequence packing (EAGLE_DRAFT_PACK=1) — eliminates padding waste on
8	#     variable-length samples; lm_head kept in bf16 by default.
9	#   * BS=4 × GRAD_ACCUM=4 = effective batch 16 (current max stable, see
10	#     bench_max_bs.py)
11	#
12	# Run directly (not in a backgrounded shell), e.g.:
13	#   bash eagle/bin/train_v2mix_20k.sh
14	#   bash eagle/bin/train_v2mix_20k.sh 2>&1 | tee /tmp/train_v2mix_20k.log
15	set -euo pipefail
16	cd /user_4813494d/openbmb
17	
18	DATA_DIR="eagle/data/target_regen/v2mix_20k"
19	OUTPUT_DIR="eagle/weights/v2mix_20k"
20	STEPS="${STEPS:-5000}"
21	EVAL_EVERY="${EVAL_EVERY:-250}"
22	
23	# Optional: validation dirs are skipped gracefully if absent (no val_ind/ood
24	# data exists yet for the merged set). Override via env if you build them.
25	VAL_IND_DIR="${VAL_IND_DIR:-eagle/data/target_regen_val/ind_200}"
26	VAL_OOD_DIR="${VAL_OOD_DIR:-eagle/data/target_regen_val/ood_bench64}"
27	
28	if [[ ! -d "${DATA_DIR}" ]]; then
29	    echo "missing data: ${DATA_DIR}" >&2
30	    exit 1
31	fi
32	mkdir -p "${OUTPUT_DIR}"
33	
34	echo "============================================================"
35	echo "  SALA draft train v2mix_20k"
36	echo "============================================================"
37	echo "  data       : ${DATA_DIR}  ($(ls "${DATA_DIR}"/*.pt 2>/dev/null | wc -l) files)"
38	echo "  output     : ${OUTPUT_DIR}"
39	echo "  steps      : ${STEPS}"
40	echo "  eval_every : ${EVAL_EVERY}"
41	echo "  val_ind    : ${VAL_IND_DIR} ($([[ -d ${VAL_IND_DIR} ]] && echo present || echo absent))"
42	echo "  val_ood    : ${VAL_OOD_DIR} ($([[ -d ${VAL_OOD_DIR} ]] && echo present || echo absent))"
43	echo "  effective batch : 4 × 4 = 16"
44	echo "  packing    : ON  (EAGLE_DRAFT_PACK=1)"
45	echo "  NVFP4 fwd  : ON  (EAGLE_NVFP4_FORWARD=1, lm_head excluded)"
46	echo "============================================================"
47	
48	export PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF="expandable_segments:True"
49	
50	# Architecture / training hyper-params (v4 sala_draft defaults; pinned here
51	# so a future env-default change does not silently shift this run).
52	export EAGLE_DRAFT_BATCH_SIZE=4
53	export EAGLE_DRAFT_GRAD_ACCUM=4
54	export EAGLE_DRAFT_SEQ_LEN_MAX=4096
55	export EAGLE_DRAFT_TTT_STEPS=3
56	
57	# Sequence packing — multiple short samples per microbatch, cross-doc isolation
58	# enforced by document_ids + per-step bool mask.
59	export EAGLE_DRAFT_PACK=1
60	export EAGLE_DRAFT_PACK_MIN_SEG=64
61	
62	# NVFP4 forward GEMM (sm_120, flashinfer cutlass backend). lm_head stays bf16.
63	export EAGLE_NVFP4_FORWARD=1
64	export EAGLE_NVFP4_EXCLUDE_LM_HEAD=1
65	
66	# Inference-side autotune cache (loaded lazily on first FP4 GEMM).
67	export SGLANG_FP4_TUNE_CACHE="/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/assets/mm_fp4_tune_sm120.json"
68	
69	python3 eagle/training/sala_draft/train.py \
70	    --steps "${STEPS}" \
71	    --eval_every "${EVAL_EVERY}" \
72	    --data_dir "${DATA_DIR}" \
73	    --val_ind_dir "${VAL_IND_DIR}" \
74	    --val_ood_dir "${VAL_OOD_DIR}" \
75	    --vocab_cache "eagle/data/vocab_cache_det_prefill.pt" \
76	    --output_dir "${OUTPUT_DIR}" \
77	    --empty_cache_every 25
78
```

```
1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	set -euo pipefail
3	
4	cd /user_4813494d/openbmb
5	
6	PROMPTS="${EAGLE_TARGET_REGEN_PROMPTS:-eagle/prompts/target_regen/v2mix_10k.jsonl}"
7	OUT_DIR="${EAGLE_TARGET_REGEN_DATA_DIR:-eagle/data/target_regen/v2mix_10k}"
8	COLLECT_DIR="${EAGLE_TARGET_REGEN_HOOK_DIR:-/tmp/eagle_target_regen}"
9	GEN_TOKENS="${EAGLE_TARGET_REGEN_TOKENS:-4095}"
10	CONCURRENCY="${EAGLE_TARGET_REGEN_CONCURRENCY:-64}"
11	EXPECTED_TPS="${EAGLE_TARGET_REGEN_EXPECTED_TPS:-2000}"
12	MIN_FREE_GB="${EAGLE_TARGET_REGEN_MIN_FREE_GB:-80}"
13	FULL_IGNORE_EOS_RATIO="${EAGLE_TARGET_REGEN_FULL_IGNORE_EOS_RATIO:-0.10}"
14	FORCE_IGNORE_EOS="${EAGLE_TARGET_REGEN_FORCE_IGNORE_EOS:-0}"
15	
16	if [[ ! -f "${PROMPTS}" ]]; then
17	  echo "missing prompts: ${PROMPTS}" >&2
18	  echo "build with: bash eagle/bin/build_target_regen_prompts.sh" >&2
19	  exit 1
20	fi
21	
22	echo "============================================================"
23	echo "  EAGLE target-regenerated collect"
24	echo "============================================================"
25	echo "  prompts      : ${PROMPTS}"
26	echo "  out_dir      : ${OUT_DIR}"
27	echo "  collect_dir  : ${COLLECT_DIR}"
28	echo "  gen_tokens   : ${GEN_TOKENS}  (saved_len=$((GEN_TOKENS + 1)))"
29	echo "  concurrency  : ${CONCURRENCY}"
30	echo "  full_ratio   : ${FULL_IGNORE_EOS_RATIO}"
31	echo "  force_ignore : ${FORCE_IGNORE_EOS}"
32	echo "  expected_tps : ${EXPECTED_TPS}"
33	echo "  min_free_gb  : ${MIN_FREE_GB}"
34	echo "============================================================"
35	df -h /user_4813494d/openbmb
36	
37	mkdir -p "${OUT_DIR}" "${COLLECT_DIR}"
38	
39	FORCE_IGNORE_EOS_ARGS=()
40	if [[ "${FORCE_IGNORE_EOS}" == "1" || "${FORCE_IGNORE_EOS}" == "true" || "${FORCE_IGNORE_EOS}" == "TRUE" ]]; then
41	  FORCE_IGNORE_EOS_ARGS=(--ignore-eos)
42	fi
43	
44	# collect.py's preflight estimates worst-case storage (every sample saturating
45	# generate-tokens). With early-EOS the real footprint is ~half (v2mix_10k:
46	# 51.8 MB/sample observed vs 96.9 MB worst-case). Set
47	# EAGLE_TARGET_REGEN_SKIP_DISK_CHECK=1 to bypass the preflight when there is
48	# ample headroom but not 2× worst-case.
49	SKIP_DISK_CHECK_ARGS=()
50	SKIP_DISK_CHECK="${EAGLE_TARGET_REGEN_SKIP_DISK_CHECK:-0}"
51	if [[ "${SKIP_DISK_CHECK}" == "1" || "${SKIP_DISK_CHECK}" == "true" || "${SKIP_DISK_CHECK}" == "TRUE" ]]; then
52	  SKIP_DISK_CHECK_ARGS=(--skip-disk-check)
53	  echo "  skip_disk_check : ON  (estimate uses worst-case full-length; relying on observed avg)"
54	fi
55	
56	python3 eagle/pipelines/target_regen/collect.py \
57	  --in "${PROMPTS}" \
58	  --out_dir "${OUT_DIR}" \
59	  --collect_dir "${COLLECT_DIR}" \
60	  --generate-tokens "${GEN_TOKENS}" \
61	  --temperature 0.0 \
62	  --full-ignore-eos-ratio "${FULL_IGNORE_EOS_RATIO}" \
63	  "${FORCE_IGNORE_EOS_ARGS[@]}" \
64	  --concurrency "${CONCURRENCY}" \
65	  --expected-tps "${EXPECTED_TPS}" \
66	  --min-free-gb "${MIN_FREE_GB}" \
67	  "${SKIP_DISK_CHECK_ARGS[@]}" \
68	  --request-timeout 7200 \
69	  --hook-timeout 180
70	
71	echo "============================================================"
72	echo "  collection complete"
73	find "${OUT_DIR}" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.pt' | wc -l
74	du -sh "${OUT_DIR}"
75	df -h /user_4813494d/openbmb
76
```

> AGENT

现在画面整齐了，给你梳理一下 `eagle/` 当前生产路线在做什么、怎么做的。

## 1. 顶层结构与基线

- `models/v2mix_20k_s3500_ood757/`：**当前生产 draft**（`eval/start_eagle.sh` 默认指向它，484 MB safetensors）。conversion_meta：step=3500，best ood0=0.7571，ind=[0.689, 0.638, 0.595]，ood=[0.757, 0.726, 0.672]。NVFP4 except `model.fc` 和 `lm_head`（bf16）。
- `models/det_prefill/`：上一代基线，best ood0=0.7530（v2mix_20k 比它高 0.4 个百分点）。配置完全一致，差别只在数据。
- `legacy/v2_v3/`、`legacy/v4/`：回滚归档，trainer 仍 `import eagle.legacy.v2_v3.train as v3train` 复用 `Eagle3Model` 父类、`load_embed_and_lm_head`、`init_mlp_from_target` 这些不变的胶水。

## 2. 数据路线：target-regenerated

不同于早期的「用预存语料做 label」，现在是**让 target 模型自己生成续写当作 label**。三段流水线：

1. **`pipelines/target_regen/build_prompts.py`**：按 60/22/11.5/5.5/1 比例从 chinese_r1 / stem_zh / open_code / codeforces / dolphin_r1 拼 chat 模板，限制 prompt 8–4096 token，要求结尾正好是 `[im_start, assistant, \n]`(73441/16434/5)。`--exclude_prompts` 支持增量扩增（10k → +10k 时去重 source_id）。
2. **`pipelines/target_regen/start_server.sh`**：起一个**带 hook 的 SGLang server**，target 模型 NVFP4，`SGLANG_ENABLE_SPEC_V2=0`（关掉投机，只做单跑生成），`EAGLE3_AUX_LAYERS=1,10,22`、`EAGLE3_TOP_K=128`，hook 把 prompt-last + 每个 decode token 的 (token_ids, aux_hidden, top128 logit values+indices, target_logsumexp, assistant_mask) 写到 `/tmp/eagle_target_regen/<rid>.pt`。
3. **`pipelines/target_regen/collect.py`**：req/save 双线程池。`rid = e3os_<full|norm>_<idx>_<N>`。10% 样本 `ignore_eos` 强制塞满 4096 token（`is_full_ignore_eos` 用 `idx*2654435761 % 10000` 决定，确定性 hash），其余正常 EOS 截断。finalize 校验 `assistant_mask[0]==False`、`assistant_mask[1:].sum()==n-1`、保存 token 与 server 返回 output_ids 严格 prefix 一致；落到 `data/target_regen/v2mix_20k/000000.pt..` （现已 19800 个文件）。

数据 budget 约 25.4 KB / saved token，10K samples × 4096 tokens ≈ 几百 GB 量级。

## 3. 模型架构（`core/`）

`core/arch.py`：单 decoder layer（hidden=4096, heads=32/2 GQA, head_dim=128, mlp=16384）+ EAGLE-3 fc(12288→4096) + lm_head(4096→32000)。两个核心点：

- **TTT 跨步 KV-cache 是 list**（`Eagle3Attention.forward`）：step 0 走 SDPA flash（`is_causal=True`），step k>0 手算 attention，把 cache_k[0] 当成对所有 query 共享、cache_k[i≥1] 当成对角项（query p 只看 cache_k[i][p]）。整体 softmax 做一次。这把 EAGLE-3 「verify-aware draft」 在训练时直接展开。
- **bf16 softmax 优化**：在 step k>0 的 (B, H, S, S+n_extra) attn_w 上不上行 fp32，注释里明确说 "max-shift 仍在内核做、只 sum/division 累在 bf16"，省了 4097-key reduce 的最大一笔显存拷贝。

`core/fp4_quant.py`：`FP4QATLinear` 继承 `nn.Linear`，训练时跑真 NVFP4 GEMM。
- `EAGLE_NVFP4_FORWARD=1`：前向调 `sgl_kernel.scaled_fp4_quant` + `flashinfer.mm_fp4(use_nvfp4=True, backend="cutlass")`，反向用 bf16 STE（`grad_x = grad_out @ w_bf16`，`grad_w = grad_out.T @ x`）。alpha = 1 / (gs_x · gs_w) 从而 weight amax 很小时不会 e4m3 underflow。
- `fp4_quant_freeze` / `unfreeze`：每个 optimizer step 把 weight 量化一次缓存，3 步 TTT 复用，不重复打包 wq/wsf。
- `EAGLE_NVFP4_EXCLUDE_LM_HEAD=1`（默认）：lm_head 走 bf16，避免 vocab projection 量化噪声漏到每个 softmax。

## 4. 训练（`training/sala_draft/train.py`）

LongSpec / SpecForge 风格，五件主要事情：

1. **AOI（Anchor-Offset Indices）**：`aoi_position_ids` 给短 shard 加随机 sink (0-4) + 随机 offset (0..AOI_CAP-S)，让 draft 在训练时见到「位置不是从 0 开始」的样本。`AOI_CAP=144000`，`ROPE_THETA=144000`，`ROPE_MAX_SEQ = AOI_CAP + SEQ_LEN_MAX*(TTT+2) ≈ 226K`。配合后续 inference 的 524K 外推。
2. **LK^λ loss（`lk_lambda_loss_topk`）**：SpecForge PR #492 的 loss——`L = λ·KL(p_target‖p_draft) + (1−λ)·(1−α)`，`λ = exp(−3·sg[α])`。**只在 target top-K（K=128）∩ draft vocab（32000）支撑集上算 KL**，没有 dense scatter 到 73448。draft 归一化用 `logsumexp(draft_logits)`，target 归一化用 `target_logsumexp`（hook 时已存）。`α = Σ min(p_on_draft, q)` 是估计的 acceptance rate，loss 只用 detach 后的 α 调权。
3. **response-only mask + cross-doc mask**：assistant_mask 来自 hook（prompt 部分=False，generated=True），loss 只在 assistant 段累。`build_attention_mask_with_docs` 把因果 + 同 doc 限制叠成 (B,1,S,S) additive mask，packed 多文档时 query 只能看自己的 segment。
4. **TTT 三步 + 0.8 衰减**：`total_loss = Σ 0.8^i · step_loss[i]`，三步 acc 都记。step k 把 input 左移 k+1 位（用预 pad 的 `ext_*` 切片，没有 cat）。packed 模式下 `q_doc` 跟着左移、最后 k 位用 `VOID_DOC = INT_MAX` 填充，对应位置 cache_k[0] 任何 doc 都不匹配，被 packed_attn_mask 屏蔽掉。
5. **vocab cache**：`eagle/data/vocab_cache_det_prefill.pt` 装 `selected_ids`（draft 词表 32000 个 target token id）+ `d2t`，trainer 构造 `t2d`（73448 bool 表「是否在 draft 词表」）和 `draft_idx_map`（73448 → 0..32000 槽位）。lm_head 初始化为 target lm_head 在 `selected_ids` 上的子矩阵。

工程优化点也都在这里：
- **`PackedFileSampler`**（`packing.py`）：FFD bin packer，按文件长度倒序贪心塞 max_len。`mask_segment_tail` 把每段尾部 ttt_steps 位 assistant_mask 置 False（左移会跨段污染）。
- **异步 prefetcher**：`_BatchPrefetcher` 后台线程跑 `make_packed_batch`（CPU + pin_memory），主线程 `to('cuda', non_blocking=True)` 走 PyTorch copy stream，跟 forward 重叠。注释说 load_wait 从 4.2s 降到 26ms，+12% 整体。
- **AdamW fused + 衰减拆分**：norm scales / biases 不衰减；fused=True 用多张量 CUDA AdamW kernel。
- **resume 状态**：`best.pt` 同时存 model + optimizer state_dict（2026-05-07 起），resume 不丢动量。
- 仅当 OOD step0 提升才覆盖 `best.pt`（`save_policy: best_only`）。

## 5. 转换为 SGLang draft（`convert_to_sglang.py`）

把训练 ckpt 重新打包成 SGLang `LlamaForCausalLMEagle3` 目录：
- `q_proj/k_proj/v_proj` 拼成 `qkv_proj`，`gate_proj/up_proj` 拼成 `gate_up_proj`，对这些**线性层调 `add_nvfp4_tensors`**（来自 legacy v2_v3）做真 NVFP4 RTN 离线打包；`fc` 和 `lm_head` 保持 bf16（与 SGLang exclude path、训练时 `_fp4_skip` 的 lm_head 行为对齐）。
- `d2t` 写成 diff 形式（`selected_ids - arange(32000)`）方便 SGLang inference 直接 add 回去。
- 写 `config.json`（`architectures=["LlamaForCausalLMEagle3"]`、`max_position_embeddings=max(524288, aoi_cap)`、`rope_theta=144000`、`eagle_config.eagle_aux_hidden_state_layer_ids=[1,10,22]`），写 `hf_quant_config.json`（`exclude_modules=["model.fc","lm_head"]`），拷贝 tokenizer。

## 6. 这条路线的差异点（vs det_prefill）

`det_prefill` 是 prefill-style 收集（prompt 完整入 prefill，逐 token 取 hidden），`v2mix_20k_s3500_ood757` 改成 **decode-style + target 自生成续写**：
- 训练样本的 sequence 全是 target 在线 decode 出来的 token（贴近推理时 draft 看到的分布），prompt 只贡献 1 个 bridge token。
- 数据扩到 19.8K（10k base + 10k extra hard-link），同 vocab cache、同超参，3500 步 OOD step0 就追平 + 略超 det_prefill 的 3000 步结果（0.757 vs 0.753）。仍在跑（log 已到 step 3503）。

## 7. 怎么串起来用

```
build_target_regen_prompts.sh → prompts/target_regen/v2mix_10k.jsonl
                              ↓
start_server.sh (target NVFP4 + hook) ← collect_target_regen.sh  →  data/target_regen/v2mix_20k/*.pt
                                                                        ↓
                                                      train_v2mix_20k.sh (LK^λ + TTT3 + AOI + pack + NVFP4 fwd)
                                                                        ↓
                                                          weights/v2mix_20k/best.pt
                                                                        ↓
                                                      convert_to_sglang.py (NVFP4 RTN)
                                                                        ↓
                                                  models/v2mix_20k_s3500_ood757/  ← start_eagle.sh

```

`start_eagle.sh` 默认拉这套 draft，配 `spec_steps=3, topk=2, dtn=7`、dynamic spec 模式（NO_SPEC bs≥31 / D7 bs≤1 / D5 中间），就是当前比赛提交栈里 spec decoding 的活动 baseline。

> DEVELOPER

看到一篇有趣的文章 仔细深入调查后 和我讨论 怎么上马 peagle 工作 EAGLE 是当前主流的投机解码方法，但自回归草稿生成带来了隐性瓶颈——投机深度越大，草稿生成的前向推理次数越多。P-EAGLE（Parallel EAGLE）将草稿生成从自回归改为单次前向并行生成，在 NVIDIA B200 上相比 vanilla EAGLE-3 实现最高 1.69x 的吞吐提升。本文介绍 P-EAGLE 的工作原理、在 vLLM 中的集成实现，以及如何使用预训练 checkpoint 快速部署。
EAGLE[1] 是目前投机解码领域的主流方法，但它的自回归草稿生成方式存在一个隐性瓶颈：投机的 token 越多，drafter 需要的前向推理次数就越多，到一定程度后额外开销就会吞噬收益。P-EAGLE 通过在单次前向推理中生成所有 K 个草稿 token，消除了这一瓶颈，在 NVIDIA B200 上的真实工作负载中实现了相比 vanilla EAGLE-3 最高 1.69x 的加速。

你只需下载（或训练）一个支持并行草稿生成的 drafter head，在 vLLM 的推理部署配置中添加 "parallel_drafting": true 即可启用。HuggingFace 上已提供 GPT-OSS 120B[2]、GPT-OSS 20B[3] 和 Qwen3-Coder 30B[4] 的预训练 P-EAGLE head。

本文将介绍 P-EAGLE 的工作原理、从 v0.16.0[5]（PR#32887）开始如何集成到 vLLM，以及如何使用预训练 checkpoint 进行部署。相关资源：

• ArXiv 论文[6]

• HuggingFace 模型（GPT-OSS 120B[2]、GPT-OSS 20B[3]、Qwen3-Coder-30B[4]）

• vLLM 集成：Unified Parallel Drafting[7]

• vLLM-Speculators（RFC[8]、PR[9]）


图 1：P-EAGLE 与其他方法在 SPEED-BENCH 上的对比，Concurrency 为 1，单卡 NVIDIA B200。
快速上手 P-EAGLE
在 SpeculativeConfig 中通过一个配置项即可启用并行草稿生成：

# vllm/config/speculative.py
   parallel_drafting: bool = True
vLLM 中启用 P-EAGLE 的示例命令：

  vllm serve openai/gpt-oss-20b \
   --speculative-config '{"method": "eagle3", "model": "amazon/gpt-oss-20b-p-eagle", "num_speculative_tokens": 5, "parallel_drafting": true}'
EAGLE 的草稿生成瓶颈
EAGLE 相比标准自回归解码可实现 2–3 倍加速，已广泛部署在 vLLM、SGLang 和 TensorRT-LLM 等生产推理框架中。EAGLE 以自回归方式生成草稿 token——生成 K 个草稿 token 需要 K 次 draft model 的前向推理。随着 drafter 模型越来越擅长生成长输出，这一草稿生成开销变得不可忽视——drafter 的延迟随投机深度线性增长，限制了投机力度的进一步提升。

核心方法：Parallel-EAGLE (P-EAGLE)
P-EAGLE 将 EAGLE 从自回归草稿生成转变为并行草稿生成。在 B200 GPU 上，P-EAGLE 在 GPT-OSS 20B 的 MT-Bench、HumanEval 和 SpeedBench 上实现了相比 vanilla EAGLE-3 1.05x–1.69x 的加速。目前已集成到 vLLM，可用于加速生产部署。

P-EAGLE 在单次前向推理中生成 K 个草稿 token。图 2 展示了其架构，包含两个步骤。

步骤 1：Prefilling。 目标模型处理 prompt 并生成一个新 token，与正常推理过程一致。同时，P-EAGLE 捕获模型的内部隐藏状态：每个 prompt 位置的 h_prompt，以及新生成 token 的 h_context。这些隐藏状态编码了目标模型在每个位置"知道"的信息，将用于指导 drafter 的预测。这一步与自回归 EAGLE 完全相同。

步骤 2：P-EAGLE Drafter。 Drafter 为每个位置并行构建输入。每个输入由 token embedding 和 hidden state 拼接而成。

对于 prompt 位置，输入将每个 prompt token embedding emb(p) 与目标模型对应的 h_prompt 配对。与自回归 EAGLE 的惯例一致，位置做了偏移——位置 i 接收位置 i-1 的 token 和 hidden state，从而预测位置 i 的 token。

对于位置 1（Next-Token-Prediction, NTP），输入将新生成的 token embedding emb(new) 与 h_context 配对。这个位置的运作方式与标准自回归 EAGLE 完全一致。对于位置 2 到 K（Multi-Token-Prediction, MTP），所需的输入——token embedding 和 hidden state——尚不存在。P-EAGLE 使用两个可学习参数来填充：一个共享的 mask token embedding emb(mask) 和一个共享的 hidden state h_shared。这些是训练过程中学到的固定向量，作为中性占位符。

所有位置一起通过 N 层 transformer 层，然后经过 language model head，在单次前向推理中预测草稿 token t1、t2、t3 和 t4。


图 2：P-EAGLE 架构概览。
在长序列上训练 P-EAGLE
现代推理模型会产生很长的输出。如图 3 所示，GPT-OSS 120B 在 UltraChat 数据集上生成的序列（含 prompt）中位长度为 3,891 token，P90 为 10,800 token。Draft model 必须在匹配的上下文长度上训练才能在推理时有效。





图 3：GPT-OSS 120B 在 UltraChat 数据集上的序列长度（prompt + 生成）分布。Reasoning level: Medium。

一个关键挑战是并行草稿生成在训练时会放大显存需求。在长度为 N 的序列上训练 K 个并行组，总共产生 N × K 个位置。当 N = 8,192、K = 8 时，单个训练样本包含 65,536 个位置。Attention 要求每个位置关注所有有效位置——65K × 65K 意味着超过 40 亿个元素，在 bf16 下消耗 8GB 显存。

Position sampling[10] 通过随机跳过位置来减少显存，但跳过太多会降低草稿质量。Gradient accumulation 是显存受限训练的标准方案，但它是在不同训练样本间拆分的。当单个序列就超出显存时，就没有东西可以拆分了。

P-EAGLE 引入了一种序列分区算法用于序列内拆分。该算法将 N × K 的位置序列划分为连续的 chunk，在 chunk 边界处维护正确的 attention 依赖关系，并在同一序列的不同 chunk 间累积梯度。详见 P-EAGLE 论文[6]。

在 vLLM 中的实现
并行草稿生成的挑战
在许多投机解码设置中，草稿生成和验证共享相同的 per-request token 布局。这对 EAGLE 基本成立：drafter 消耗的窗口已经匹配验证器将检查的内容——K 个草稿 token 加上一个额外采样的 token。

并行草稿生成打破了这种一致性。为了在一次 drafter 前向推理中预测 K 个 token，需要追加 MASK 占位符（例如 [token, MASK, MASK, …]）。这些额外位置仅用于草稿生成，因此 draft batch 的形状不再匹配 verification batch 的形状。由于无法复用验证阶段的 metadata，必须重建 batch metadata——扩展输入 token ID、hidden state 和 position 以插入 mask token/embedding 的 slot，递增每个 request 的 position，然后从更新后的 position 重新计算 slot mapping 和 per-request start indices。

Triton Kernel
为了抵消重建 batch metadata 的开销，实现了一个融合的 Triton kernel，在 GPU 上通过复制和扩展目标模型的 batch 来填充 drafter 的输入 batch。在一次 pass 中，kernel 将之前的 token ID 和 position 从目标 batch 复制到新的目标 slot，并插入目标模型采样的 per-request bonus token。然后用特殊的 MASK token ID 填充额外的并行草稿 slot。最后生成轻量级 metadata：rejected-token mask、并行草稿 slot 的 masked-token mask、用于采样草稿 token 的 new-token indices，以及 hidden-state mapping。

这些逻辑如果不融合，需要多个 GPU op（copy/scatter + insert + fill + mask + remap）。将它们融合到一个 kernel 中减少了 launch 开销和额外显存流量，使草稿生成的准备工作开销很低。

Hidden State 管理
对于将 hidden state 传递给 draft model 的 EAGLE 类方法，并行草稿生成会单独处理这些字段的填充。由于 hidden state 比输入 batch 的其余部分大得多，工作被拆分：Triton kernel 输出一个 mapping，然后一个专用的 copy kernel 将学到的 hidden state 占位符广播到 mask token slot 中。

# 将目标 hidden state 复制到新位置
 self.hidden_states[out_hidden_state_mapping] = target_hidden_states

# 用学到的 Parallel Drafting hidden state 填充 masked 位置
 mask = self.is_masked_token_mask[:total_num_output_tokens]
 torch.where(
     mask.unsqueeze(1),
     self.parallel_drafting_hidden_state_tensor,
     self.hidden_states[:total_num_output_tokens],
     out=self.hidden_states[:total_num_output_tokens],
 )
parallel_drafting_hidden_state_tensor 从模型的 mask_hidden buffer 中加载，这是一个学到的表示，告诉模型这些位置应该预测未来的 token。

对于 KV cache slot mapping，有效 token 接收正常的 slot 分配，rejected token 被映射到 PADDING_SLOT_ID（-1）以防止错误的 cache 写入。对于 CUDA graphs，capture range 被扩展了 K × max_num_seqs 以容纳并行草稿生成引入的更大 draft batch。

vLLM 上的 P-EAGLE 基准测试
在 GPT-OSS-20B 上训练 P-EAGLE，并在三个基准上评估：MT-Bench[11]（多轮指令跟随）、SPEED-Bench Code（长代码生成）和 HumanEval[12]（函数级代码合成）。P-EAGLE 在低并发（c=1）下实现 55–69% 的吞吐提升，在高并发（c=64）下保持 5–25% 的增益，对比对象为公开可用的 vanilla EAGLE-3 checkpoint[13]。结果见图 4-6。

P-EAGLE drafter 是一个轻量的 4 层模型，训练目标是并行预测最多 10 个 token。为评估性能，扫描了投机深度 K ∈ {3,5,7} 和并发级别 C ∈ {1,2,4,8,16,32,64}。目标是为 P-EAGLE 和 vanilla EAGLE-3 找到正确的部署配置。两者均使用 linear drafting。"best P-EAGLE" 和 "best EAGLE-3" 指在给定投机深度 K 下达到峰值吞吐（tokens per second, TPS）的配置。

一个一致的模式出现了：P-EAGLE 在所有并发级别下都在 K=7 达到峰值 TPS。而 vanilla EAGLE-3 在 K=3 达到最高 TPS，偶尔在不同并发下偏向更高值。这反映了并行草稿生成的根本优势——P-EAGLE 在单次前向推理中生成所有 K 个草稿 token，允许它在不增加额外序列开销的情况下从更深的投机中获益。而自回归 drafter 必须逐步生成投机 token，限制了其高效扩展到更大 K 的能力。

所有实验在单卡 NVIDIA B200（Blackwell）GPU 上使用 vLLM 进行，部署配置如下：

VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_MOE_MXFP4_MXFP8=1 \
vllm serve openai/gpt-oss-20b \
    --speculative-config '{
      "method": "eagle3",
      "model": "amazon/GPT-OSS-20B-P-EAGLE",
      "num_speculative_tokens": 7,
      "parallel_drafting": true}' \
    --port 8000 \
    --max-num-seqs 1024 \
    --max-model-len 100000 \
    --max-num-batched-tokens 100000 \
    --max-cudagraph-capture-size 4096 \
    --no-enable-prefix-caching \
    --no-enable-chunked-prefill \
    --kv-cache-dtype fp8 \
    --async-scheduling \
    --stream-interval 20
注意： 目前在 vLLM 上部署带 EAGLE drafter 的 GPT-OSS-20B 需要一个单行 patch（PR#36684[14]）。请在启动前应用。该修复预计将在后续 vLLM 版本中合入。

图 4：P-EAGLE 与 EAGLE-3 在 GPT-OSS-20B 上不同并发级别下的 MT-Bench 吞吐（TPS）。P/E 加速比：1.55x（c=1）、1.29x（c=2）、1.35x（c=4）、1.28x（c=8）、1.27x（c=16）、1.09x（c=32）、1.05x（c=64）。

图 5：P-EAGLE 与 EAGLE-3 在 GPT-OSS-20B 上不同并发级别下的 HumanEval 吞吐（TPS）。P/E 加速比：1.55x（c=1）、1.53x（c=2）、1.45x（c=4）、1.35x（c=8）、1.31x（c=16）、1.37x（c=32）、1.23x（c=64）。

图 6：P-EAGLE 与 EAGLE-3 在 GPT-OSS-20B 上不同并发级别下的 Speed-bench 吞吐（TPS）。P/E 加速比：1.69x（c=1）、1.61x（c=2）、1.54x（c=4）、1.45x（c=8）、1.40x（c=16）、1.22x（c=32）、1.25x（c=64）。
除了减少草稿生成开销，P-EAGLE 的吞吐提升还来自更好的 Acceptance Length（AL），即验证器每轮投机中接受的平均草稿 token 数。更高的 AL 意味着更多的草稿工作转化为实际输出，直接提升有效 OTPS/TPS。

以下表格对比了 P-EAGLE 和 vanilla EAGLE-3 在 GPT-OSS-20B 上三个基准的 AL：

P-EAGLE (AL):

Config    HumanEval    SPEED-Bench    MT-Bench
K=3    3.02    2.87    2.87
K=7    3.94    3.38    3.70
EAGLE-3 (AL):

Config    HumanEval    SPEED-Bench    MT-Bench
K=3    2.65    2.24    2.70
K=7    3.03    2.59    3.27
P-EAGLE 在相同投机深度 K 下始终获得比 EAGLE-3 更高的 AL。在 K=7 时，P-EAGLE 在 HumanEval 上比 EAGLE-3 高 30%（3.94 vs 3.03），在 SPEED-Bench 上高 31%（3.38 vs 2.59），在 MT-Bench 上高 13%（3.70 vs 3.27）。值得注意的是，P-EAGLE 从更深投机中获益更多：从 K=3 到 K=7，P-EAGLE 的 AL 在 HumanEval 上增加 0.92（3.02 到 3.94），而 EAGLE-3 仅增加 0.38（2.65 到 3.03）。这一在更高 K 时不断扩大的差距，与 P-EAGLE 的单次并行草稿生成一致——更深的投机不会带来额外开销。

复现结果
启动服务后，使用 vllm bench serve 运行基准测试：

#MT-Bench
export MODEL="openai/gpt-oss-20b"
export BASE_URL="http://localhost:8000"
vllm bench serve \
    --dataset-name hf \
    --dataset-path philschmid/mt-bench \
    --num-prompts 80 \
    --max-concurrency 1 \
    --model $MODEL \
    --base-url $BASE_URL \
    --temperature 0.0 \
    --hf-output-len 2048

#HumanEval command:
#Download HumanEval dataset openai/openai_humaneval
vllm bench serve \
    --dataset-name custom \
    --dataset-path <dataset path> \
    --num-prompts 164 \
    --max-concurrency 1 \
    --model $MODEL \
    --base-url $BASE_URL \
    --temperature 0.0 \
    --custom-output-len 2048
结论
P-EAGLE 消除了投机解码中的序列化瓶颈，在真实工作负载上实现了相比 vanilla EAGLE-3 最高 1.69x 的加速。通过将草稿数量与前向推理次数解耦，我们现在可以探索更大的草稿架构，甚至能实现比单层基线更高的接受率。该实现通过手写融合 kernel 精细处理了输入准备、attention metadata 管理和 KV cache slot mapping 的复杂性。虽然需要专门训练的模型，但性能收益使其成为 vLLM 投机解码能力的重要补充。

随着更多并行训练模型的推出，这种方法有望成为生产 LLM 部署的优选。P-EAGLE 的架构效率与 vLLM 的基础设施相结合，为追求最大推理性能和更低延迟的场景提供了清晰的路径。

现在就试试：从 HuggingFace 下载预训练的 P-EAGLE head，在 vLLM 配置中设置 "parallel_drafting": true，体验加速效果。

> AGENT

我把文章对照我们当前的栈仔细看了一遍，给你结论 + 上马路线。

## 核心判断

**值得做 spike，但不能直接搬。** 文章给的所有集成都在 vLLM；我们跑的是 demo-sala/sglang fork，光 EAGLE 相关 spec worker 就 ~2900 行（`eagle_worker.py` 1836 行 + `eagle_worker_v2.py` 838 行 + `eagle_utils.py` 205 行），P-EAGLE 在 SGLang 侧目前没有 reference 实现，整条推理路径要自己改。

## 与我们栈的关键差异（决定收益上限）

1. **draft 容量不同**：GPT-OSS P-EAGLE 用的是 4 层 drafter，论文也明说"轻量 4 层"。我们 SALA draft 是 1 层 NVFP4。1 层在 K=7 上做并行 mask 预测是不是表达力够，是个开放问题——位置 6、7 的 acceptance 可能反而比 autoregressive 差。
2. **硬件不同**：B200 算力大，draft 自回归的多次 launch overhead 占比放大；6000D 上单次 draft forward 绝对时间更长，**节省 K-1 次 forward 的相对收益没 B200 那么戏剧化**。
3. **K 自适应已经在做**：`start_eagle.sh` 里 dynamic spec mode（NO_SPEC bs≥31 / D7 bs≤1 / D5 中间）+ MARS theta 已经在 batch-size 维度做 K=3↔6 切换。P-EAGLE 的核心卖点"加 K 不加成本"在 D5 区间（K=3）有提升空间，但 D7（K=6）已经在跑了，比较点不像 vanilla EAGLE-3 那么悬殊。
4. **训练数据已就位**：`v2mix_20k` decode-style target-regenerated 4096 长，跟论文要求的"长 reasoning 序列训练"对齐；现有 `lk_lambda_loss_topk` 也能直接套到 K 个并行位置上。

## 推荐三步走

**Step 0 — 不写代码，先量数**（半天）：
跑当前 `bench/mini_bench.sh` + nsys profile，量出 draft K=3 自回归在总 decode 时间里占多少。如果 < 20%，P-EAGLE 上限就只有 ~5%，不值得动 SGLang。MARS dynamic 把 high-bs 都 NO_SPEC 了，**真正的甜点就是 c=1 reasoning 长输出场景**。

**Step 1 — 训练侧 spike**（1-2 天）：
在 `eagle/core/arch.py` 加 `mask_emb`、`mask_hidden` 两个 learnable buffer，把 `Eagle3DecoderLayer` forward 从 TTT-3 自回归展开改成单次 K 并行（query 全部一次 attend cache_k[0]，不再有 list-based KV）。用现有 `v2mix_20k` ckpt 做 `--resume` 短 fine-tune（500-1000 步 + LR 衰减），看 AL 能否在 K=7 接近论文的 ~30% 提升。SEQ_LEN=4096、K=8 时 N×K=32K，attn 大约 2 GB bf16，单 GPU 能装，**不需要论文那个 chunked partitioning**。

**Step 2 — 推理侧 offline micro-bench**（半天）：
不上 SGLang，写个 PyTorch 脚本：load fine-tuned draft + load target NVFP4，构造 `[token, MASK, MASK, ...]`，把 K=3/5/7 的 P-EAGLE draft + greedy verify 跟现有 K=3 自回归在 toolkit/eval_dataset 上对比 AL。这步是 go/no-go 关。

**Step 3（go 才做）— SGLang 集成**：
改 `eagle_worker.py` 的 draft step 路径：input_ids 插 K-1 个 MASK、position 顺延、hidden_states 用 `mask_hidden` 填新 slot、KV slot mapping 把 mask 位 → PADDING(-1)、CUDA graph capture 范围扩 K×max_running。还要重新跑 `tune_mm_fp4_sm120.py`——draft 一次 K 个 query 把 m 维从 1 变到 7，b12x 派发可能落到不同 tile 配置。MARS theta 跟"一次出 K 个 candidate"的 prune 语义需要重新对齐。**这一步是大头**，没有 step 1/2 的数据不要起。

## 主要风险

- **AL 不一定换得到 TPS**：论文 c=64 只剩 1.05x；我们提交云端不知道实际 batch 分布，如果偏高 bs，收益会被 dynamic NO_SPEC 吃掉。
- **MARS 互操作**：现在 D5/D7 的 theta 是针对 chain verify 调的，P-EAGLE 改了 draft 出 token 的 logits 分布形状，theta 大概率要重调，且 D5/D7 的最优 K 会变。
- **lock-in 风险**：当前 v2mix_20k_s3500_ood757 + EAGLE-3 是 commit `091060d` 明确 "lock-in production decode +28.5%"。要替换得在 e2e bench 上证明严格优于现状，AL 提升不是充分条件。

要不要我先起 Step 0 的 profile？需要先跑一次 mini_bench 配合 nsys 看 draft 占比。

```
{"body":"### Checklist\n\n- [x] If this is not a feature request but a general question, please start a discussion at https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/discussions. Otherwise, it will be closed.\n\n### Motivation\n\nWe propose SPECTRE (Parallel **Spec**ulative Decoding with a Multi-**T**enant **Re**mote Drafter), a novel system architecture for Speculative Decoding in SGLang , inspired by recent parallel speculative-decoding efforts such as [Pearl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.11850) and [SSD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03251). By decoupling the tightly-bound Drafter and Verifier into independent, asynchronously communicating SGLang services (via ZMQ), SPECTRE resolves the high sequential latency and poor resource utilization of traditional implementations through two core features:\n\n**Cross-Round Parallel Overlap**: Breaking the strict, synchronous \"draft-then-verify\" loop that leads to low GPU utilization, SPECTRE employs optimistic concurrency control. Assuming high draft acceptance, the Drafter generates tokens for round k+1 concurrently while the Verifier validates round k. This completely overlaps the forward passes of both models and effectively hides drafting latency.\n\n**Drafter Dual-Mode Coexistence**: Instead of dedicating an underutilized GPU solely to the Drafter, SPECTRE treats it as a multi-tenant service. It processes internal draft requests from the Verifier alongside its own standalone user traffic within the same continuous forward batch. This maximizes GPU utilization and drastically lowers the serving cost for production environments.\n\n**paper link**: (coming soon...)\n\n## Architecture Overview\n\nTo break free from this co-located synchronous trap, SPECTRE shifts speculative decoding into a **distributed, asynchronously coordinated pipeline**.\n\nSPECTRE keeps both sides operationally familiar:\n\n- The **Verifier** is a standard SGLang HTTP server that handles user requests and performs token verification with the large model. It acts as the authoritative serving endpoint.\n- The **Drafter** is also a standard SGLang HTTP server. However, operating in **Dual-Mode**, it accepts remote draft-generation requests from the Verifier *in addition* to serving its own standalone traffic. Both traffic streams are mixed into the same GPU batch.\n- The two sides communicate over **ZMQ**, with messages serialized as `SpectreRequest` objects through a C++ pybind11 transport layer.\n\n![Image](https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/36f76fc6-df8d-431e-a47c-4c46ee6a8220)\n\nAs shown in the image above, SPECTRE can achieve overlap between the verifier and drafter computation results. Furthermore, drafter can simultaneously support the computation of both verifier requests and normal requests.\n\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Footprint\n\nSPECTRE is implemented against SGLang `0.5.10rc0`. Most new code is isolated under `python/sglang/srt/speculative/spectre/`, with only a small set of integration points in the existing serving stack.\n\n```text\npython/sglang/srt/speculative/spectre/\n|\n|- spectre_protocol.py              # SpectreRequest, SpectreAction, SpecType\n|- spectre_communication.py         # SpectreConfig, SpectreZMQCommunicator\n|\n|- verifier/\n|  |- spectre_target_scheduler_mixin.py   # SpectreTargetSchedulerMixin, DraftCircuitBreaker\n|  `- spectre_worker.py                   # SpectreWorker\n|\n|- drafter/\n|  |- spectre_draft_scheduler_mixin.py    # SpectreDraftSchedulerMixin\n|  |- spectre_state_manager.py            # SpectreDraftState, SpectreDraftStateManager\n|  `- spectre_kv_rollbacker.py            # SpectreKVRollbacker\n|\n`- cpp_zmq/\n   |- src/\n   |  |- spectre_zmq.cpp\n   |  `- spectre_zmq_serialization.cpp\n   `- setup.py\n```\n\n### Main integration points in the existing codebase\n\n```text\nsrt/server_args.py\n    spectre_* CLI arguments and ServerArgs fields\n\nsrt/speculative/spec_info.py\n    SPECTRE detection, worker import, and routing to the SPECTRE event loop\n\nsrt/managers/scheduler.py\n    scheduler mixin integration, init_spectre_communication(),\n    event_loop_normal_spectre_draft/target(), and state reset on flush\n\nsrt/model_executor/cuda_graph_runner.py\n    spectre_ntpb_options for CUDA graph batch-size variants\n```\n\nThe C++ ZMQ extension is built once before launch:\n\n```bash\nbash python/sglang/srt/speculative/spectre/cpp_zmq/scripts/build_cpp_zmq.sh\n```\n\n---\n\n\n## Protocol Design\n\nAll communication uses a `SpectreRequest` dataclass serialized through msgpack via the C++ ZMQ layer.\n\n### Key fields\n\n\n| Field              | Type            | Description                                                                                  |\n| ------------------ | --------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `request_id`       | `str`           | SGLang request ID (`req.rid`)                                                                |\n| `spec_cnt`         | `int`           | Per-request speculative round counter used to correlate messages and discard stale responses |\n| `action`           | `SpectreAction` | `DRAFT`, `FINISH`, `ABORT`, or `REJECT`                                                      |\n| `spec_type`        | `SpecType`      | `DRAFT_REQUEST` or `DRAFT_RESPONSE`                                                          |\n| `draft_token_ids`  | `List[int]`     | Draft tokens proposed by the Drafter                                                         |\n| `input_ids`        | `List[int]`     | Full prompt token IDs, sent on `spec_cnt == 0`                                               |\n| `output_ids`       | `List[int]`     | Tokens generated so far                                                                      |\n| `num_draft_tokens` | `int`           | Number of draft tokens requested by the Verifier                                             |\n\n\n### Action semantics\n\n\n| `SpectreAction` | Direction                                   | Meaning                                             |\n| --------------- | ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |\n| `DRAFT`         | Verifier -> Drafter and Drafter -> Verifier | Normal draft request / response                     |\n| `FINISH`        | Verifier -> Drafter                         | Request completed normally; Drafter cleans up state |\n| `ABORT`         | Verifier -> Drafter                         | Request aborted; Drafter cleans up state            |\n| `REJECT`        | Drafter -> Verifier                         | Drafter is overloaded; Verifier should back off     |\n\n\n---\n\n## Transport Layer\n\n### ZMQ transport selection\n\n`SpectreConfig` chooses the transport automatically based on `--spectre-zmq-addr`:\n\n\n| `zmq-addr` value         | Transport | Rationale                                                             |\n| ------------------------ | --------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `127.0.0.1` or `0.0.0.0` | **IPC**   | Avoids unnecessary network stack overhead for same-machine deployment |\n| Any other IP             | **TCP**   | Enables cross-machine communication                                   |\n\n\n### Socket roles\n\nThe current design uses:\n\n- **Verifier**: binds a **Router** socket\n- **Drafter**: connects with a **Dealer** socket\n\nThis choice keeps the Verifier in a coordination role and makes it possible to extend toward one-to-many topologies, where a single Verifier can talk to multiple Drafter instances.\n\nThat matters because SPECTRE is intended to scale outward cleanly. The transport is not just point-to-point glue; it is the foundation for a future multi-drafter deployment model.\n\n### C++ transport layer\n\nSPECTRE uses a C++ pybind11 extension for ZMQ plus msgpack serialization. This keeps message handling predictable and efficient in the serving path, avoiding Python-level control flow overhead for every transport operation. The communication layer is kept thin, explicit, and production-friendly — it is not an additional infrastructure dependency but a narrow binding on top of well-established primitives.\n\n---\n\n## Scheduling, Fault Tolerance, and State Management\n\n### Circuit breaker on the Verifier\n\nThe Verifier maintains a `DraftCircuitBreaker` so that Drafter failures do not stall user-visible generation.\n\n\n| State       | Behavior                                                                                                              |\n| ----------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `CLOSED`    | Normal operation. Draft requests are sent every round.                                                                |\n| `OPEN`      | Too many consecutive timeouts. The Verifier falls back to single-token decoding for `SPECTRE_COOLDOWN_ROUNDS` rounds. |\n| `HALF_OPEN` | Cooldown expired. One probe request is sent. Success returns to `CLOSED`; failure reopens the breaker.                |\n\n\nThis gives SPECTRE a critical operational property: when remote drafting is unhealthy, the system degrades to standard autoregressive decoding instead of amplifying latency or deadlocking progress. SPECTRE accelerates the happy path without holding the serving stack hostage to the remote path.\n\n### Drafter scheduling modes\n\nBecause draft tokens are highly time-sensitive, the Drafter exposes two scheduling strategies.\n\n**Mixed mode** (default):\n\n- Draft requests are merged into the Drafter's normal running batch.\n- This maximizes overall GPU utilization.\n- It works well when Drafter load is light or moderate.\n- It offers no strong isolation, so under heavy load the Verifier may see occasional timeouts.\n\n**Draft-Priority mode** (`--spectre-draft-priority`):\n\n- The Drafter runs a dedicated draft batch for up to `--spectre-max-draft-priority-steps` steps before serving normal requests each iteration.\n- This bounds draft latency more tightly.\n- It protects Verifier throughput when the Drafter is busy.\n- The trade-off is slightly higher tail latency for the Drafter's ordinary user traffic.\n\nThis mode split reflects an actual serving trade-off rather than hiding it: SPECTRE can optimize for utilization or for speculative responsiveness, depending on deployment goals.\n\n---\n\n## User-Facing API\n\n### CLI arguments\n\n#### Shared arguments\n\n\n| Argument                   | Type  | Default | Description                                                           |\n| -------------------------- | ----- | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `--spectre-role`           | `str` | `None`  | `target` for the Verifier or `draft` for the Drafter                  |\n| `--spectre-zmq-addr`       | `str` | `None`  | ZMQ address. `127.0.0.1` selects IPC automatically; other IPs use TCP |\n| `--spectre-zmq-port`       | `str` | `None`  | ZMQ port used by both sides                                           |\n| `--spectre-max-batch-size` | `int` | `32`    | Overload threshold for sending `REJECT`                               |\n\n\n#### Verifier-side arguments\n\n\n| Argument                         | Type    | Default | Description                                                                                 |\n| -------------------------------- | ------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `--speculative-algorithm`        | `str`   | `None`  | Must be `SPECTRE`                                                                           |\n| `--speculative-num-steps`        | `int`   | `None`  | Draft tree depth
```
